# QT Homie 254 — Corrected Target Treatments

**Founder directive:** Homie owns semantic reading and treatment. Cody implements; Cody does not reinterpret target meaning.

## Governing discipline

For every chair: use stored Backend Authority and publisher facts, read the complete preserved target face, retain every explicit printed semantic supported by the evidence, add only truthful SCZN3 measured/session intelligence, and invent no unpublished timing, sequence, qualification threshold, penalty, or course-of-fire rule. Existing registered geometry is implementation evidence, not proof that it matches the face. Where geometry conflicts with the face, the face and governed semantics win and the geometry must be corrected. RESULT → SEC → VAULT → exact reopen must preserve the same evidence, coordinates, classification, and distribution.

**Treatment counts:** Zone / Object Counting: 154, Evidence Only: 63, Scoring: 36, Zeroing: 1 = 254.

**Founder-found universal implementation checks:** readable RESULT; truthful tie language; tap-coordinate fidelity; Vault source photograph; preserved image aspect; Vault zone/scoring breakdown; target fit; complete artwork semantics; correct region registration. The previously removed Founder-link/reopen-navigation item is not included as a product defect.
